The caravan route is clear. For now. But questions linger like smoke after battle.
The deepwoods are full of valuable fungi. Learn to spot the safe ones, and you can sell your finds to the inn or traders passing through.
The fire dims but does not die. Where once you conquered through might, now you endure through wisdom. Each step costs more than it once did. The question is no longer 'Can I?' but 'Should I?' Perhaps it is time to rest.
The old militia captain's been wanting to upgrade the grounds for some time now. You could pitch in coin.
The scars tell their stories now. Your sword arm is as sharp as ever, but it aches in the morning. Experience compensates for what youth provided freely. You have seen much, survived more. The years weigh heavier.
